## Stage6 LlamaFactory

- DirectGitHub clone succeeded, no proxy: https://github.com/hiyouga/LLaMA-Factory.git tagv0.9.3 commit `ca75f1edf3cb50343ed1c98605141c3e22075b5f`.
- Training venv inherits existingTorch2.9.1 binaries; local Transformers4.52.4/PEFT0.15.2/datasets3.6.0/accelerate1.7.0/TRL0.9.6. No vLLM environment mutations. Install logs:logs/stage6_train_environment_install.log.
- PyArrow26 requiredNumPy2, conflicting with LlamaFactory NumPy<2; pinPyArrow19.0.1. ExistingCLI unnecessarily imports inheritedvLLM0.16 with oldTransformers; use supported trainingAPI, no chat engine.
